STATE BANK OF INDIA 
(CENTRAL OFFICE) 

Bombay, the 4th January 1987 

SBD No, 1/1987,—It is hereby notified for general informa¬ 
tion that in pursuance of clause (d) of sub-section (1) of SJec- 
tioD 25 of the State Bank of India (Subsidiary Banks) Act, 
1959, (38 of 1959), the Slate B.ank of India, in consultation 
with the Reserve Bank of India, hereby nominates Dr. K. S. 
Uixidhyay. 1-19, Saraswii/Jii Colony, Behind Rnjyalnkshini 
The.atre, Ring Road, Uppal, Hyderabad (A.P.), as a Director 
of the State Bank of Hyderabad for a term of three years 
from the 27th January, 1987 to the 26th Jaflniaiy, 1990 (both 
days inclusive) in place of late Dr. (Miss) Dollie M. Shenoy. 

(Sd.) ILLEGIBLE 
Chairman 


STATE BANK OF TRAVANCORE 
(Associate of the State Bank of India)

NOTICE 

Trivandrum, the 30tb> January 1987 

No. MD42/160.—-The Twenty-Seventh Annual General 
Meeting of the Shareholders of State Bank of Travancorc 
will be held in the Auditorium attached to the Staff Training 
Centre at the Hank’s Administrative Complex at Foojapura, 
Trivandrum on Thursday the 19th March. 1987 at 5.00 PM 
(Standard Time) for the transaction of the followine busi¬ 
ness : 

‘To receive the Report of the Board of Directors, the 
Balance Sheet and Profit and Loss Account of the Bank 
jnade upto the 31st December, 1986 and the Auditor’s 
report on the Balance Sheet and Accounts”, 

B. GUPTA

(HEADOFFICE).

inclusive).

INDIAN AIRLINES 
New Delhi, the 30th January 1987 

No. FIN/RULES/37/1366.—^In exercise of the powers 
conferred by clause (b) of sub-section (i) of section 45, 
read with sub-section (2) of section 8 of the Air Corpora¬ 
tion Act, 1953 (27 of 1953), the Indian Airlines with the 
previous approval of the Central Government, makes the 
foHowina regulations further to amend the Indian Airlines 
(Flying Crew) Service Regulations, 1959, namely :— 

1. These regulations may be called the Indian Airlines 
(Flying Crew) Service (Amendment) Regulations, 
1986. 

2. They shall come into force with effect from Ist 
January, 1987. 

3. In the Indian Airlines (Flying Crew) Service Regu¬ 
lations, 1959, in sub-para (1) of Regulation 124A, 
sub-clauses (a), (b) & (c) published in the Gazette 
of India, Part m, Section 4 dated 7th January, 1978, 
the following shall he subitUuted, namely :— 

Resulalhn 124A(1) 

(a) the encashment of Privilege Leave shall be allowed 
at the option of the employees. 

(b) the maximum number of days for which leave can 
be encashed shall be half of the Privilege leave 
lying at the credit of an employee on the date of 
encashment reduced by one year’s entitlement to be 
retained, at the credit of the employee. 

(c) the rate of encashment per day shall be calculated 
on the basis of the emoluments specified in condi¬ 
tion (d) and drawn by an employee in the month 
preceding the month in which he applied for encash¬ 
ment, divided by thirty, 

4. The existing sub-clauses (f), (g) & (li) in sub-para (1) 
of Regulation 124A, may be deleted and sub-clauses (i), 
(j), (k) & (1) may be re-numbered as sub-clauses (f), (g), 
(h) & (i). 

No. FTN/RULES/37/1366.—In exercise of the powers 
conferred by clause (b) of sub-section (2) of section 45, 
read with sub-section (2) of section 8 of the Air Corpora¬ 
tion Act, 1953 (27 of 1953), the Indian Airlines with the 
previous approval of the Central Govenunent, makes the 
following regulations farther to amend the Indian Airlines 
Aircraft Enghreoring Department Service Regulations, 1959, 
namely :— 

1. These regulations may bo called the Indian Airlines 
Aircraft Enginetering Department Service (Amendment) 
Regulations, 1986. 

2. They shall come into force with effect from Ist 
January, 1987. 

3. In the Indian Airlines Aircraft Engineering Depart¬ 

ment Service Regulations, 1959, in sub-para (1) of 
of Regulation 124A, sub-clauses (a), (b) & (c) 

published in the Gazette of India, Part III, Section 4 


dated 7th January, 1978, the following shall be 
substituted, namely :— 

Regulation 1 24j4 (1) 

(a) the encasluncnt of Privilege Leave shall be allowed 
at the option of the employees. 

(b) the maximum number of days for which leave can 
be encashed shall be half of the Privilege leave 
lying at the credit of an employee on the date of 
eJJcashment reduced by one year’s entitlement to be 
retained at the credit of Uie employee. 

(c) the rate of encashment per day shall bo calculated 
on the basis of the emoluments specified in condi¬ 
tion (d) and drawn by an employee in the month 
preceding the month in which he applied for encash¬ 
ment, divided by thirty. 

4. The existing sub-clauses (f), (g) & (h) in sub-para (1) 
of Regulation 124A, may be deleted and sub-clauses (i), 
(j), (k) & (1) may be re-numbered as sub-clauses (f), (g), 
(h) & (i). 

Np. FIN/RULES/37/1366.—In exercise of the powers 
conferred by clause (b) of sub-section (2) of section 45, 
read with sub-section (2) of section 8 of the Air Corpora¬ 
tion Act, 1953 (27 of 1953), the Indian Airlines with the 
previous approval of the Central Government, makes the 
following regulations further to amend the Indian Airlines 
(Employees other than Flying Crew and those in the Aircraft 
Engineering Department) Service Regulations, 1959, 
namely :— 

1. These regulations may be called the Indian Airlines 
(Employees other than Flying Crew and those in the 
Aircraft Engineering Department) Service (Amend¬ 
ment) Regulations, 1986. 

2. They shall come into force with effect from 1st 
January, 1987. 

3. In the Indian Airlines (Employees other than Flying 
Crew and those in the Aircraft Engineering Department) Ser¬ 
vice Regulations, 1959, in sub-para (1) of Regulation 124A. 
sub-clauses (a), (b) and (e) published, in the Gazette of 
India, Part III, Section 4 dated 7th January, 1978, the follow¬ 
ing shall be substituted, namely :—^ 

Regulation 124zl(l) 

(a) the encashment of Privilege Leave shall he allowed 
at the option of the employees. 

(b) the maximum number of days for which leave can 
be encashed shall be half of the Privilege leave 
lying at the credit of an employee on the date of 
encashment reduced by one year’s entitlement to be 
retained at the credit of the employee, 

(c) the rate of encashment per day shall he calculated 
on the basis of the emoluments specified in condi¬ 
tion (d) and drawn by an employee in the month 
preceding the month in which he applied for encash¬ 
ment, divided by thirty, 

4. The existing sub-clauses (f), (g) & (h) in sub-para (1) 
of Regulation 124A, may be deleted and sub-clauscs (i), 
(j). (k) & (I) may he re-numbered as sub-clauses (f), (g), 
(h) & (i).
